  function LimoniAppsLoadMomentFormat(callback) {

                  <a href="{{products.results_url}}" class="header-search__see-more button button--secondary" data-results-count="{{products.results_count}}">{{products.results_label}}</a>
                {{else}}
                  <p class="header-search__no-results h4">{{products.results_label}}</p>
                {{/if}}
              
            </div>

            <div class="grid__cell 2/5--handheld 2/6--lap 1/4--desk">
              
                <p class="header-search__category text--uppercase">Pages</p>
              

              
                {{#if has_others_results}}
                  <ul class="header-search__pages list--unstyled">
                    {{#each others.results}}
                      <li>
                        <a href="{{url}}" class="header-search__page link link--secondary link--effect">{{title}}</a>
                      </li>
                    {{/each}}

                  <a href="{{others.results_url}}" class="header-search__see-more button button--secondary" data-results-count="{{others.results_count}}">{{others.results_label}}</a>

<script type="text/javascript">
  goTop();
  function goTop() {
    var scollTop = $(window).scrollTop()
    if(scollTop > 70){
      $('#go-top').fadeIn();
    }
    if(scollTop == 0){
      $('#go-top').fadeOut();
    }
  }
  $(window).scroll(function() {
    goTop()
  });
  $('#go-top').on('click',function() {
    $('html , body').animate({scrollTop: 0},'slow')
  })
</script></div><script src="//cdn.shopify.com/s/javascripts/currencies.js"></script><script src="//cdn.shopify.com/s/files/1/0225/2960/5706/t/4/assets/libs.js?6397"></script>
